A hoisted vi.mock() of a CommonJS package ends with its test file: the next test file reads the package itself.
#### a.test.js
import { filler1 } from "./filler.mjs"; void filler1;
import { test, vi } from "bun:test";
import { seen } from "./src/uses.js";
vi.mock("pkg", () => ({ default: { f: () => "mocked" }, f: () => "mocked" }));
test("a", () => console.log("| CASE", "a " + seen()));
#### b.test.js
import { filler1 } from "./filler.mjs"; void filler1;
import { test } from "bun:test";
import { seen } from "./src/uses.js";
test("b", () => console.log("| CASE", "b " + seen()));
